## Phase 40: Live red-flag extraction quality (measure-first) — M8

The Phase-38 entity playbook applied to RED FLAGS: measure → context-shape → gate-harden → verify-only-if-residue, with a human checkpoint between measure and fix. The live companion's flag extraction passes the deterministic faithfulness/grounding gate, but completeness / span quality / translation register / consistency are UNGUARDED (no structured prompt guidance, no second pass) — the user sees inconsistent flags in real live tests. Direction user-set at the Phase 39 delivery gate; assumption gate closed 2026-06-09 (A1/A2 accept round 1; A3/A4 reject→revised→accept round 2; ledger block in assumption-ledger.md). Confined to the live subsystem + the shared gate; NO client/news.html change; dists stay byte-identical; NO non-negotiable change. Measurement = deterministic proxies + a BLIND second-rater reference extraction reported as INTER-RATER AGREEMENT (consensus, NEVER ground truth — the Phase 34/38 method); gate extensions are measurement-EARNED and land in the SHARED news_ground.py (companion + build.py); captured stress outputs LOCAL-ONLY; fixture promotion US-federal-only (Phase-39 compliance posture).

> Abort: an extended gate check that fails a committed record is a FINDING to adjudicate, never a silent loosening; if holdout degrades under the new prompt, roll the prompt back and report (don't tune on holdout); if the blind second-rater design can't yield a meaningful agreement signal, present that honestly at the T2 checkpoint rather than inventing a metric; blocked >3 attempts on a task → mark [blocked: …] + ask the user: skip or abort.

## Phase 39: Live news QOL — streamed extraction progress + one-shot URL acquisition

QOL upgrade for the LIVE news subsystem (companion-served, dev/authoring-time only). HALF 1 (PROGRESS): POST /extract becomes a chunked NDJSON stream of stage events (received → [fetching → converting] → extracting → grounding → verifying i/N → done {payload}); `serve_news.extract()` gains an optional `on_progress=None` callback so the Phase-38 replay-fixture seam (call_llm stubbing, deterministic build_record) is untouched; the client reads via fetch+ReadableStream and renders stage label + verify i/N + elapsed timer in the live region. NO token streaming (raw JSON isn't user-meaningful + misses the verify loop, the wall-time majority). Designed fallback if stdlib chunked flush misbehaves under ThreadingHTTPServer: job-id + polling (verify at impl). HALF 2 (URL ACQUISITION, ONE-SHOT — the user rejected preview-then-run): /extract accepts {url} OR {text}; a NEW companion-only `scripts/news_fetch.py` owns acquisition — a multi-method fetch LADDER (urllib with browser-like headers → curl subprocess → markitdown convert(url); bot guards expected) + a deterministic format STANDARDIZER (strip nav/link-list/image boilerplate from converted markdown, collapse whitespace) + a VERIFIER gate (article-shape checks: min prose length/ratio, bot-guard/captcha/paywall/JS-wall detection → honest structured failure suggesting paste). markitdown stays `.venv`-only via lazy import + graceful degrade (the news_store/DuckDB pattern). The converted text streams back in an early event and fills the textarea as extraction runs — passive recovery: trim + re-run. Acquisition PROPOSES, the scripted gate DISPOSES (the project spine, applied to fetch). INVARIANTS: all new client code inside `/*LIVE_START*/…/*LIVE_END*/` → offline dist/news BYTE-IDENTICAL (`--check news` zero drift); build.py NEVER imports the live layer (news_fetch companion-only); the always-on badge stays; NO non-negotiable change; replay fixtures stay green WITHOUT re-capture. Direction approved at the assumption gate 2026-06-09.

## Phase 38: Consolidate the live news subsystem (verify agent backend + watchlist management)

CONSOLIDATE the Phase-35/36 LIVE news subsystem (companion-served, dev/authoring-time only) — the user's choice at the dev-plan gate over extending source scale (FINTRAC /intel/ depth or a third jurisdiction, both DEFERRED). Two halves, BOTH companion-only; the OFFLINE `dist/news` stays BYTE-FROZEN (the live + watchlist-view code lives in the build-time-STRIPPED `/*LIVE_START*/…/*LIVE_END*/` region; build.py NEVER imports the live/store layer `serve_news`/`news_store` — it DOES import `news_ground`, the SHARED stdlib grounding primitives, by design so live grounding == build grounding). NEW FACT that reframes the phase: the Qwen model (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, llama.cpp, OpenAI-compatible) is LIVE on 127.0.0.1:8080, and a planning smoke already confirmed the live backend end-to-end — one committed article → 4 entities + 5 grounded red flags in 17.6s, with the deterministic grounding gate correctly DROPPING a 6th ungrounded (ellipsis) flag. So this is a CAPTURE-AND-LOCK phase, not a fix-broken-backend phase. **HALF A (the centerpiece, the user's stated intent) — verify + lock the agent backend.** `call_llm` (the model step) is the ONLY thing the test suite currently stubs (a hand-written CANNED dict); everything downstream (`parse_llm_json` → `build_record` → ground) is already covered. So: run the real Qwen over the committed DOJ/OFAC articles, CAPTURE each RAW model output as a committed fixture under `tests/fixtures/news-live/`, add OFFLINE replay tests (parse→build→ground over the fixtures, dep-free, no model) pinning the real model's output contract incl. the ungrounded-elision drop, + an OPT-IN `--live` smoke that hits 8080 but is OFF the default offline run (the `.venv`-gated DuckDB pattern). EVALUATION is TWO-LAYER + honesty-constrained: (1) deterministic GROUNDING = faithfulness (automatic, catches hallucination); (2) a CLAUDE-REFERENCE comparison = quality (completeness/correctness) — Claude extracts the same articles → a reference (grounded by the SAME gate) → report Qwen-vs-Claude as INTER-RATER AGREEMENT (consensus, NEVER Claude-as-ground-truth) + the actual divergences, for the user to adjudicate; NO accuracy/precision/recall number presented as real; the always-on "Illustrative data & outputs" badge stays (mirrors the Phase 34/37 agreement-as-consensus + human-adjudication method; grounding gate ≠ completeness gate ≠ correctness gate). **HALF B — the watchlist-management VIEW + prune** (completes the Phase-36 feedback loop, currently invisible: the escalated-only watchlist only GROWS; `GET /watchlist` serves it but there is no view/prune UI). Add a `news_store` prune/un-escalate method, a `serve_news` prune route, and a `news.html` LIVE-REGION watchlist panel (view the escalated surface with provenance + a prune control). Stripped offline; `dist/news` byte-identical. 6 lite tasks T1–T6 (no L; T2 = a deterministic entity-precision filter added mid-phase per the user's T1-checkpoint adjudication). FROZEN byte-clean: the showcase (index.html + config/** + 3 typology dists), the ENTIRE corpus (corpus.html + dist/corpus + all source dirs + every derived record + the 2 overlays), `derive_signals.py`, AND the OFFLINE `dist/news` (byte-identical via the strip). NO non-negotiable change. Direction approved by user at the goal gate 2026-06-08. Decisions D1–D3 in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

## Phase 37: Per-indicator typology (corpus Typologies lens)

The corpus Typologies lens groups each document by ONE money-laundering typology (`data/typology-map.json`, doc→typology, a 27-term closed-vocab overlay) — a Phase-24 honesty constraint (indicators are NOT matched/de-duped across regulators; the doc→one-typology overlay is traceable to each doc's stated subject). That model breaks on the FINTRAC per-sector guidance pages: a sector page (e.g. *Financial entities*) enumerates indicators across MANY typologies — bribery/corruption, TF, structuring-below-threshold, wire transfers, non-Canadian jurisdictions — so 7 of the 10 derived sector pages couldn't take a single real typology and were bucketed into a catch-all `fintrac-sector-baselines`. The user, eyeballing the BUILT demo, asked why "only 7 documents" show under the FINTRAC guidance typology. Investigation (this session): all 10 derived sector pages RENDER in `dist/corpus` — 3 are filed under their own typology (casinos→`casino-gaming`, real-estate→`real-estate`, virtual-currency→`virtual-currency`), and all 10 appear together in the **Documents** lens under "FINTRAC sector guidance (ML/TF indicators)". Nothing is lost. But the catch-all presents FINTRAC's richest contribution as a vague lump in the PRIMARY cross-corpus (Typologies) view. The source side is COMPLETE: FINTRAC publishes exactly 11 per-sector `indicators-indicateurs/<sector>_mltf-eng` pages (we have all 11; 10 derived, crown-agents honestly skipped); the STR-reporting + sanctions-evasion guidance pages enumerate NO indicators (process guidance, honestly non-derivable). The remaining derivable FINTRAC frontier is in the /intel/ special-bulletin family (OA001 tax-evasion-real-estate + sanctions-evasion SB + Russia-linked-ML SB + dual-use advisory) — offered and DEFERRED in favour of the representation fix.

The user chose the PROPER fix (over a lite relabel of the catch-all, and over adding the /intel/ scale): tag typology PER INDICATOR so a sector page's indicators distribute across the real typology clusters and `fintrac-sector-baselines` retires. Method (the project's established grain — MEASURE → assign-per-unique-text → verify-as-AGREEMENT → human-adjudicate → byte-surgical → regate; overlay-validated at the build boundary; derived records + grounding core stay BYTE-FROZEN). T0 RISK that shapes the phase: many indicators are genuinely CROSS-CUTTING ("cash deposits below the reporting threshold" is structuring, which serves drug-trafficking AND fraud AND TF), so per-indicator-ONE-typology may merely RELOCATE the catch-all to indicator level — big neural effort, marginal gain. Therefore step 1 MEASURES before any tagging and CHECKPOINTS the model choice with the user (single primary typology / multi-label set / an honest indicator-level "sector-general" bucket) and the deterministic[section-heading]/neural split. The overlay (`data/indicator-typology-map.json`, indicator-id→typology) keeps ALL 56 derived records + `derive_signals.py` BYTE-FROZEN — at build time an indicator's typology = overlay value, else INHERITS its doc's typology-map value (the ~1,231 single-typology indicators inherit for free; only the ~1,020 sector-page indicators, ~589 unique texts, need the neural assignment). HONESTY carries forward: combined coverage stays honest union arithmetic over the per-indicator statuses, NO lift/similarity/cross-regulator dedup is computed or claimed, the always-on "Illustrative data & outputs" badge stays. T2 (assign/verify) is a strong Workflow candidate (the Phase 33/34 fan-out) — the user opts in at implementation. 5 lite tasks T1–T5 (one L = T2). FROZEN byte-clean: the showcase (index.html + config/** + 3 typology dists), the entire news stream (news.html + dist/news + data/news/** + serve_news/news_store/news_ground), ALL 56 derived records, the grounding core `derive_signals.py`, `data/capability-taxonomy.json`. NO non-negotiable change. Direction approved by user at the goal gate 2026-06-08. Decisions D1–D2 in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

- [x] T1 · Measure-first probe + model CHECKPOINT — a deterministic analysis (no tagging) over the 7 catch-all sector pages (`fintrac-guid-{financial-entities,msb,securities,life-insurance,accountants,bc-notaries,dpms}`): enumerate their indicators (~1,020), group by UNIQUE `flag` text (~589 expected), map each indicator to its source SECTION heading, and per section heading score its typology-determinism (cleanly→one existing-vocab typology, e.g. "ML indicators related to bribery and corruption"→`corruption`, "Indicators specifically related to TF"→`terrorist-financing`) vs cross-cutting/agnostic; list candidate closed-vocab extensions. Present a CHECKPOINT to the user → records the MODEL (single / multi-label / sector-general bucket) + the deterministic/neural split + whether the 3 specific sector pages (casinos/real-estate/VC) are folded in for consistency. | scope: .dev-wiki/tmp/**, data/fintrac-guidance/derived/** | success: `test -f .dev-wiki/tmp/ph37_probe.md` — the report enumerates the unique-text count + a section→typology determinism table + the cross-cutting fraction + vocab-extension candidates; the user has chosen the model (recorded in the report). NO `data/indicator-typology-map.json` written yet (`! test -f data/indicator-typology-map.json`). | size: M

## Phase 36: Persistence (DuckDB→parquet) + feedback watchlist — M8

Wire the Phase-35 M8 news-stream live companion to a persistence layer + a feedback WATCHLIST, all companion/venv-side with ZERO ship-path impact. The loop is ESCALATED-ONLY (the user's choice at the gate, REFINING D4's "book ∪ all prior-scanned"): (1) a live scan → grounded record → the companion PERSISTS the scan to DuckDB (disposition NULL); (2) the Screen step scores each extracted entity against book ∪ watchlist(escalated) — real client-side Jaro-Winkler, UNCHANGED; (3) at the Disposition gate, ESCALATE posts back → DuckDB marks `disposition='escalate'` → that entity JOINS the watchlist; (4) a later article screens against the now-larger surface — a re-mentioned escalated entity hits with provenance "escalated from <prior article>, <date>". Rationale (D1): a domain-honest CURATED watchlist (not a dump of incidental names that degrades screening precision), it makes the existing human Disposition gate CONSEQUENTIAL (escalate → future scans catch it), and it closes the "working instance of the loop, not a dramatization" arc Phase 35 named. Mechanism is pre-decided (D2): a NEW `scripts/news_store.py` OWNS DuckDB (schema scans / entities[+disposition] / red_flags; native `COPY … (FORMAT parquet)` export); build.py NEVER imports it (the no-authoring/no-store-layer invariant holds). Watchlist reconciliation: book rows `{role,country,segment}` and scanned entities `{location,age,profession,context}` both COLLAPSE to a uniform screening row `{name,type,kind:'book'|'scanned',provenance}` — `name` is what the matcher scores; `provenance` keeps the exposure view honest about WHY a hit fired. Byte-identity holds: a new `GET /watchlist` + `POST /disposition` are COMPANION-ONLY; the client watchlist/disposition/screen-union wiring lives INSIDE `/*LIVE_START*/…/*LIVE_END*/` so `render_news` STRIPS it → dist/news byte-identical, zero store/network code offline; static offline screening stays book-only. Escalation may target ANY extracted entity (proactive watchlisting), not only book-matches. DuckDB installs into the existing gitignored uv `.venv` (where markitdown lives — companion/authoring-time only); the store file + parquet exports are gitignored (same convention as `data/*/raw/`). The companion DEGRADES gracefully if duckdb is missing (still serves the page + `/extract`; persistence disabled with a warning). HONESTY (D3): real client-side fuzzy scores, synthetic book, the always-on illustrative badge stays, prior-scanned watchlist entries are REAL grounded extractions, nothing shown that isn't grounded in a submitted source; escalation provenance shown in the screen/exposure view. ZERO ship-path impact (DuckDB venv-only, never ship). NO non-negotiable change. Direction approved by user at the goal gate 2026-06-08 (escalated-only feedback watchlist + DuckDB→parquet persistence). Decisions D1–D3 in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

Install duckdb into the existing `.venv`. build.py must NOT import it. Serialize writes (a single-writer connection / lock — ThreadingHTTPServer appends are concurrent). | scope: scripts/news_store.py | success: `.venv/bin/python -c "import duckdb"` && `.venv/bin/python scripts/news_store.py --selftest` (a temp DuckDB: append → set disposition → watchlist union book∪escalated with provenance → parquet export roundtrip) && `python3 -c "assert 'news_store' not in open('scripts/build.py').read(), 'build.py must not import news_store'"` | size: M

## Phase 35: News live local-model backend — M8

Signal Watch's M8 news stream today does extraction at BUILD/AUTHORING time (`derive_signals`-style; `build.py validate_news_data` grounds every entity/attribute/red-flag against the source, then inlines a frozen dataset into `dist/news`). The user steered the phase off the queued C/D-quality candidates to evolve M8: hook the news stream to a REAL local-hosted model (llama-cpp serving a Qwen ~30B-A3B-class MoE) for REAL-TIME, on-demand extraction — paste/pick an article → the live model extracts entities + red flags + the AML translation → the existing client-side fuzzy screen runs against the book → human disposition → exposure. This is the long-anticipated "live mode" the non-negotiable already sanctions ("optional, isolated, off by default, always has a scripted fallback") — finally built, scoped to news. ARCHITECTURE (3 user choices at the direction gate): (1) SERVED-BY-COMPANION — a thin stdlib Python companion (`scripts/serve_news.py`) serves `news.html` over http://localhost AND proxies llama-cpp; same-origin → NO CORS/mixed-content. (2) The offline single-file `dist/news` stays the default + scripted fallback, BYTE-IDENTICAL: the live branch lives in a marked `/*LIVE_START*/…/*LIVE_END*/` region that `render_news` STRIPS for the offline build, so the offline file keeps ZERO network code and the existing self-contained `die("fetch(")` guard holds; the companion serves the template whole. (3) GROUNDING STAYS IN PYTHON server-side — a shared `news_ground.py` (stdlib normalize + raw/normalize-substring grounding + the article-body transform), reused by BOTH build.py's gate AND the companion; live model output is JSON-schema-constrained then DETERMINISTICALLY GROUNDED (ungrounded entities/flags DROP) — the "LLM proposes, the deterministic gate disposes" spine, moved to runtime. NOT re-ported to JS (the companion choice makes server-side grounding the clean answer; revised from the direction-gate's initial "port to JS" framing). Model integration is via llama-cpp's OpenAI-compatible `/v1/chat/completions` (model swappable; thinking disabled / `<think>` stripped defensively). Phase 35 = the live-extraction BACKBONE; it screens against the STATIC `NEWS.book`. PERSISTENCE (companion → DuckDB row-append → parquet export) + the FEEDBACK WATCHLIST (book ∪ all prior-scanned entities, so the screen surface grows) is Phase 36 (DuckDB is the one new dep, then). Real-time = on-demand SUBMIT (paste/pick), not a continuous feed (a feed/poller is a later extension). ZERO new pip deps this phase (stdlib + urllib). HONESTY: real client-side fuzzy scores, synthetic book, the always-on illustrative badge stays, nothing shown that isn't grounded in the submitted source. NOTE — `news_ground.py` is stdlib grounding PRIMITIVES (the same kind build.py already inlines as `_news_normalize`), NOT the authoring/LLM layer: build.py STILL never imports derive_signals / markitdown / an LLM client. config.md flagged "revisit to standard ceremony if a real live-backend in M4" — this is that phase; kept lite (Jake's low-ceremony posture; the self-check gate + the byte-identical-offline invariant are the guards). FROZEN byte-clean: the showcase (index.html + config/** + 3 typology dists), the ENTIRE corpus (corpus.html + dist/corpus + all source dirs + every derived record + data/typology-map.json + data/capability-taxonomy.json), the grounding core derive_signals.py, and `dist/news/index.html` (byte-identical via the strip). NO non-negotiable change. Direction approved by user at the goal gate 2026-06-08. Decisions in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

## Phase 34: C/D-assignment verification pass — M7

Phase 33 tripled the corpus to 2,251 indicators, but the 1,376 NEW ones (61% of the corpus, in the 14 new derived records — 4 new FinCEN advisories + 10 FINTRAC-guidance sector pages) carry capability(C1-C28) / data_source(D1-D20) codes that were the ONE neural step in Phase 33, gated ONLY for vocabulary VALIDITY (in-set; `ph33_apply.py` reported 0 flagged), NEVER for CORRECTNESS. Those C/D codes drive every downstream field — status, data, build_rec, build_logic — AND the Phase-29 capability lens + Phase-30 data-source lens (the demo's executive views). A mis-assigned code silently shows a WRONG posture in the primary demo's executive views, against the honest-grounding thesis. The grounding gate (`derive_signals.py` check_record / normalize / rf_region) is about FAITHFULNESS of the verbatim flag, NOT recall/correctness of the C/D tagging — this phase audits the dimension the gate never checked (memory: "a grounding gate ≠ a completeness gate; audit recall, not just faithfulness"). Phase 34 verifies and corrects the 1,376 new C/D assignments, MEASURE-FIRST and HUMAN-adjudicated, in four moves: (1) a DETERMINISTIC consistency audit first (no LLM, cheap, sizes the problem) — same-text-different-code contradictions + within-section outliers; (2) a BLIND neural re-assignment (the measurement) — a workflow re-assigns C/D to each indicator BLIND to the existing code, deterministically compared → an INTER-RATER AGREEMENT rate + a disagreement list, reported HONESTLY as agreement (consensus), NEVER as "proven correct" (the key honesty guard against a second-LLM pass becoming false confidence); (3) HUMAN adjudication (the gate) — disagreements + contradictions CLUSTERED by (existing→proposed) code-pair/pattern so the user disposes at the cluster level interview-style (the user is the accepted truth source, same as the Phase-28 interview), not row by row; (4) a BYTE-SURGICAL apply reusing `ph33_apply.py`'s deterministic downstream — a corrected C/D code mechanically regenerates status/data/build_rec/build_logic (do NOT re-author build_logic neurally), the grounded `flag` and `red_flag` staying BYTE-IDENTICAL. This is a DATA-correctness phase: only the 14 new derived records change; `corpus.html` and `build.py` are data-driven so corrected codes flow through on rebuild — NO UI feature work. The measured agreement rate is a journal/quality artifact, NOT a new demo number (the always-on illustrative badge stays the only claim — no false precision). SCOPE = the 14 new derived records only (1,376 new indicators); the 42 existing records / 875 old indicators stay FROZEN (they had the fuller Phase-28 interview treatment) — folding them in is a follow-up ONLY IF T2's measured rate comes back ugly. FROZEN byte-clean: the showcase (index.html + config/** + 3 typology dists), the entire news stream, data/typology-map.json, data/capability-taxonomy.json (taxonomy DEFINITIONS unchanged — only which code an indicator carries moves), the grounding core derive_signals.py, build.py, and the 42 existing records / 875 old indicators. NO non-negotiable change. Direction approved by user at the goal gate 2026-06-08. Decisions in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

Emit a report listing every contradiction + outlier. ## Phase 33: Corpus completeness + full typology re-segmentation — M7

The corpus is the primary demo (Phase 27). The user reviewed the BUILT corpus and found the SOURCE SET incomplete — a DIFFERENT defect from Phase-28's within-doc extraction-completeness: there, each doc shipped too few of its own flags; here, the corpus is missing whole DOCUMENTS. Research confirmed two distinct gaps. (1) FinCEN = a discovery-WINDOW miss — the crawler floor is 2020 and there's been no re-crawl since 2026-03, so the LATEST advisory (FIN-2026-A002, 18 red flags) and the 2018-19 back-catalog (FIN-2019-A006 the original US fentanyl advisory / A005 BEC / A003 CVC / FIN-2018-A003 PEP, 14 red flags) are absent; the superseded 2020 COVID cluster is skipped. (2) FINTRAC = a source-AREA miss — all 10 current FINTRAC docs are /intel/ strategic intelligence; ZERO come from /guidance-directives/, the 11 sector "ML/TF indicators" pages (Financial entities master + MSB + real estate + securities + life insurance + DPMS + casinos + accountants + BC notaries + Crown agents + virtual currency), 50-100+ indicators each, HEAVY cross-page shared-spine duplication, HTML not PDF. Phase 33 closes both via a DEDICATED DERIVATION WORKFLOW (the user's call — "a dedicated dynamic workflow"; the acquire→convert→derive→ground fan-out over ~16 new docs is exactly a Workflow), then re-segments the typology axis across the doubled corpus (a full 22-term vocabulary review; add trade-based-money-laundering, split out of export-control-evasion / sanctions-evasion / OFAC maritime where the doc's subject warrants). The user chose MAXIMAL on both axes at the goal gate (FINTRAC = all 11 sector pages over financial-entities-master-only / master+distinctive-tails; typology = a full vocab review over TBML-targeted) — consistent with the prioritizes-scale-over-showcase-polish posture. ARCHITECTURE: new FINTRAC guidance lives in a NEW `data/fintrac-guidance/` dir + a NEW `CORPUS_SOURCES` registry entry (the Phase-20 multi-source pattern — keeps the OA dir `data/fintrac/` + its 10 derived records BYTE-FROZEN); a new HTML→md acquisition path (guidance pages are HTML, not the Phase-22 `<page>.pdf` sibling); the grounding core `derive_signals.py` is touched ONLY IF the guidance heading form doesn't ground under the existing `_RF_HEADER_FINTRAC` anchors, and ONLY as a regression-gated rf_region anchor add (the Phase-21/22/23 precedent — every existing rf_region byte-unchanged, all 42 existing records still `--check-derived` clean). New-indicator coverage posture is INHERITED-or-INTERVIEWED, NEVER fabricated: new indicators inherit status/data/build_rec deterministically from their capability(C1-C28)/data_source(D1-D20) code via the user's Phase-28 interview posture + the cover×data matrix; the workflow FLAGS any indicator that maps to no existing C/D code, and flagged ones are resolved by a short targeted y/n/partial interview that extends `data/capability-taxonomy.json` with GROUNDED posture (zero fabricated posture — ground-judgments-in-a-user-interview + honesty-over-demo-drama). Dedup honesty: the 11 FINTRAC sector pages share a common spine; they're ingested as DISTINCT per-doc publications (each separately attributed), the overlap DISCLOSED, NEVER claimed as cross-source de-duplication (the Phase-24 honesty gate — indicators are not matched/de-duped across sources, which would need fabricated matching); within-doc duplicates removed byte-surgically (the Phase-28 precedent). Both compliance bases are already established (FinCEN US-federal §105 public-domain + FINTRAC Crown-copyright non-commercial-with-attribution) → NO non-negotiable change. Direction approved by user at the goal gate 2026-06-08. Decisions in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

## Phase 32: News stream — real gov-enforcement adverse-media source + presentation elevation — M8

The Phase-31 news stream (`dist/news`) proved the muscle (synthetic news → grounded entity + red-flag extraction → client-side Jaro-Winkler fuzzy match vs a synthetic book → exposure → human disposition) but the user reviewed the BUILT artifact and judged it "very low effort … a poorly staged slide show … you are repeating the same mistakes" — the same staged-render failure called out on the corpus in Phase 27 (a RESULT, never a PROCESS). Phase 32 elevates it on TWO fronts at once, the six-act showcase AND the entire corpus staying BYTE-FROZEN. (1) REAL-SOURCE SWITCH — replace the synthetic fictional articles with REAL US-federal gov-enforcement adverse media (DOJ press releases / indictments, OFAC sanctions designations, Treasury-FinCEN enforcement, SEC litigation releases on ML & sanctions cases), public domain under 17 U.S.C. §105 — the corpus's EXACT verbatim basis (extended FinCEN→US-federal in Phase 21 for OFAC), so verbatim-clean, real named cases, lowest defamation surface (official public-record action), the MOST AML-relevant adverse media. NOT a non-negotiable change: the existing US-federal verbatim basis applied to a NEW artifact. The COUNTERPARTY BOOK STAYS SYNTHETIC (non-negotiable #4 held) — the bridge is REAL adverse-media entity × SYNTHETIC book, honestly more compelling than fictional-vs-fictional. Acquisition is build-time/authoring ONLY (WebSearch/WebFetch the gov URLs → committed verbatim md with a provenance header: source URL + public-domain basis); the ship artifact stays offline, no fetch/LLM/keys. (2) PRESENTATION ELEVATION to the corpus's bar — make the screening PROCESS legible: port the corpus's streaming "agent reading" Read render (source streams in, caret + scroll-follow, each red-flag phrase highlights as the read reaches it, the TYPOLOGY + ENTITY CARDS reveal alongside, counts up from 0) so "what was extracted" is explicit (red flags + typology + entities); entity cards carry name + location + age + profession, each QUOTE-GROUNDED in the real article (DOJ releases routinely carry "X, 45, of Miami, a licensed attorney…" so the attributes ARE groundable — drop any attribute that can't ground, never fabricate); replace the Screen jump-cut with a VISIBLE SCAN PROCESS (each book row scored REAL Jaro-Winkler in a sweep, ranked into exposure, threshold line, near-matches surfaced, the common-name trap flagged for the human); adopt the FULL corpus dossier theme (news.html currently hits ~17 theme tokens vs corpus ~36 — the styling "doesn't match") + clear per-stage labeling. The runtime stays a pure client-side matcher (normalize → token-sort → Jaro-Winkler, REAL scores, offline). HONESTY: entity attributes ground or drop; the scan shows REAL computed scores (no fake progress bar); the book synthetic; the always-on "Illustrative data & outputs" badge stays (the scenario/book/exposure is illustrative, the source articles real — the corpus's own real-source-under-illustrative-badge model); source attribution rendered like the corpus. The M8 north star (compose the adverse-media atom with the transaction-signal atom) stays scoped OUT. Direction approved by user at the goal gate 2026-06-08 (news elevation + real gov-enforcement source switch; "Gov enforcement — verbatim" over paraphrased-commercial / verbatim-commercial-news; the user's "ignore copyright, non-commercial" reframed to the clean public-domain path). ## Phase 31: M8 walking skeleton — the adverse-media / negative-news stream (a second atom stream) — M8

Signal Watch today is the advisory→signal loop (the six-act showcase + the corpus explorer). Phase 31 opens a SECOND atom stream — adverse-media / negative-news screening — as a new standalone offline artifact `dist/news/` (from a new `news.html` template, mirroring how `dist/corpus` was added). The thesis is unchanged: an adverse-media hit is an ATOM that composes with the transaction-signal atom (entity that tripped a signal AND has adverse media AND near-matches a counterparty = composite risk). The demo already opens on "the missed-monitoring / TD anxiety made visual" (HANDOFF §1.1); TD Bank's $3.09B 2024 penalty was a CDD/adverse-media failure, so a negative-news stream is that anxiety made concrete. The wiki backs the gap: ~80% of investigative intelligence is in UNSTRUCTURED text transaction systems can't touch; entity-resolution fragmentation ("John D. Smith / J.D. Smith / J Smith") + the >90% false-positive wall are the core failure modes — exactly the fuzzy-match-then-human-disposition beat. PHASE SCOPE = the THINNEST end-to-end slice that proves the ONE new muscle: unstructured news → grounded entity + red-flag extraction → fuzzy-match vs a SYNTHETIC client/counterparty book → potential exposure → human disposition. The "compose with the transaction signal" payoff is the M8 north star — NAMED, scoped OUT. User decisions at the goal gate (2026-06-08): news source = SYNTHETIC illustrative (fictional entities — controllable, zero defamation/copyright surface, lets the near-match + false-positive trap be designed in so the beat lands honestly under the badge); shape = walking skeleton as a new standalone artifact (not folded into the corpus; not full-M8-now). ARCHITECTURE reuses the corpus discipline + adds ONE runtime piece: build-time committed `data/news/{articles/*.md, derived/*.json, book.json}` (entities + red-flags QUOTE-GROUNDED in the article via normalize-substring + a natural-AML `red_flag` translation; the book seeded with a near-match + an exact match + a false-positive trap + clean rows), validated at the BUILD BOUNDARY (`validate_news_data` in build.py — the grounding core `derive_signals.py` stays BYTE-FROZEN); runtime = a pure client-side fuzzy name matcher (normalize → token-sort → Jaro-Winkler, REAL scores, thresholded). HONESTY: synthetic data under the always-on badge; fuzzy scores real, not fabricated; counts honest; the near-match/trap teach the mechanism, not claimed as detection stats. Direction approved by user at the goal gate 2026-06-08. Decisions in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

## Phase 30: Data-source lens — surface the D1–D20 data-source axis as an institution coverage-by-data-source view — M7

The corpus explorer (dist/corpus, the primary demo) gained a CAPABILITY LENS in Phase 29 (the C1–C28 axis). Phase 30 adds the symmetric DATA-SOURCE LENS on the D1–D20 axis — coverage answered PER DATA SOURCE across the whole corpus: "across every regulator-published red flag, here are the data feeds the indicators depend on, ranked by demand, cross-referenced against what the institution can actually access." Where the capability lens asks "do we have the detection CAPABILITY" (a build problem), the data-source lens asks "do we even have the DATA FEED" (an access/vendor problem). The distinct payoff: 7 of the 20 data sources have posture "n" (not yet available) — those are exactly the SOURCE_DATA indicators currently buried per-doc; the lens makes them corpus-wide legible ("here are all the indicators, across every regulator, you can't act on until you acquire e.g. blockchain analytics / beneficial-ownership data"). KEY SCOPE FACT — the BACKEND IS ALREADY BUILT (Phase 29): `data/capability-taxonomy.json` already carries the `data_sources` block (20 entries {id,name,desc,posture}; posture 9 y / 4 partial / 7 n); `build.py` already loads + referential-integrity-validates `data_source` codes (`validate_capability_taxonomy` enforces every live indicator's `data_source` resolves) and already inlines `taxonomy.data_sources` + the per-indicator `data_source` codes into `__CORPUS__`; `corpus.html` already has `DS_BY` / `DSRC` / `dsNum` helpers (used inside `renderCapability`). So Phase 30 needs ZERO change to build.py, the taxonomy, or any of the 42 derived records — it is a `corpus.html` MIRROR of the capability mode onto the D-axis, plus harness + docs + dist rebuild. HONESTY MODEL = the Phase-24 / Phase-29 re-projection precedent: per-data-source DEMAND = honest count of corpus indicators carrying that `data_source` code; institution POSTURE (have/partial/gap) = the interview answers re-grouped by data source; covered/partial/gap split = honest counts over existing per-indicator status; NO similarity/overlap/lift number; the always-on "Illustrative data & outputs" badge stays. Data sources have an empty `group` field, so D-cards omit the group line (20 flat sources need no sub-grouping). Design choice: a 4th co-equal Select mode (Documents / Typologies / Capabilities / Data sources), NOT a cross-tab inside the capability view — mirrors C exactly and gives the data-access story its own front door. Direction approved by user at the goal gate 2026-06-08 (D-lens only; OSFI disposed as non-derivable; no clean second Canadian indicator-source exists). Decisions in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

Per data source with ≥1 corpus indicator render a card: name, institution posture (have/partial/gap from the interview), # indicators demanding it, and the covered/partial/gap coverage breakdown — sorted GAP-priority (not-yet first, then partial, then in-place, then by demand). Add the data-source-mode copy variants (count line, lead paragraph, honesty framenote, h2). D-cards OMIT the group line (data sources have empty group). Honest counts only; the always-on badge stays. | scope: corpus.html | success: `python3 scripts/build.py corpus` exits 0 AND a "Data sources</button>" toggle marker is greppable in dist/corpus/index.html AND `node tests/corpus-explorer.test.mjs` still passes (no regression) | size: M

## Phase 28: Corpus COMPLETENESS + grounded-coverage interview + methodical render + branding/compliance — M7

The user reviewed the BUILT Phase-27 corpus and found the CORE defect Phase-27's assessment MISSED: verbatim red-flag EXTRACTION is grossly INCOMPLETE — the grounding gate only ever verified each extracted flag was REAL, never that we got them ALL. Phase 26 marked the corpus demo "showcase quality" (reviewer 10/10) but the user judges the BUILT artifact NOT shippable. Named letdowns on the real dist: (1) the Read-advisory extract/translate beat is "brutally bad"; (2) the build animation is "not in place" unlike the six-act showcase. User instinct: almost forego the grounding system, rely on LLM extraction+translation fully. INLINE DIAGNOSIS (evidence): the brutality is PRESENTATION not grounding — the red_flag translations are already register-quality + the grounded flags are real sentences; what reads brutal is the left "Source document" panel rendering RAW markitdown (page headers / footnote-digit runs / hyphen-wraps / table soup) + best-effort highlighting that MISSES many flags, and `renderSignal`'s build-log never adds the showcase's `.run` "working" pulse (just ticks ✓). PUSHBACK (held): forego-the-gate risks fabricated "verbatim" quotes (a compliance non-negotiable) AND wouldn't fix the raw-article render. DIRECTION (user-chosen at the gate): ASSESS-ONLY FIRST, THEN REPLAN — run the assessment, bring back per-doc findings + a recommended fix tier, then plan the build from evidence. ## Phase 25: Corpus output quality — extract → translate (natural AML red flags) + the article-processing page — M7

The corpus explorer's "red flags" are bare VERBATIM article extractions (the grounded `flag` substring), not how an AML program writes red flags — and the corpus demo lacks the showcase's article-processing beat (the showcase renders the full advisory, highlights the red-flag phrases, then extracts/translates). This phase brings the corpus demo to the showcase's two-layer model: keep step 1 = the grounded verbatim extraction (the evidence), ADD step 2 = a TRANSLATION into a natural AML-term red flag. Each live derived indicator gains a `red_flag` field BESIDE its existing verbatim `flag`. A new per-doc screen renders the FULL source article, highlights the grounded phrases (FREE — they are exact substrings by the Phase-16 grounding invariant), then reveals the translation. THE LOAD-BEARING HONESTY MODEL: the verbatim `flag` stays the grounded authority, shown BESIDE the `red_flag` (never replaced); the grounding gate logic stays BYTE-UNCHANGED; the new gate check is SHAPE only (present / non-empty / distinct-from-verbatim / length-bounded) — translation faithfulness is the one NEURAL step, mitigated by show-both + the always-on illustrative badge + the EFE oracle (T1) + per-doc re-check. Paraphrase is the compliance DEFAULT, so the translation ALIGNS with the non-negotiables (NO non-negotiable change). The full article renders verbatim under each source's existing basis (US-federal public-domain 17 U.S.C. §105 / FINTRAC Crown-copyright non-commercial licence). Direction approved by user 2026-06-07 (full re-derive now over exemplar-first / presentation-only; corpus extract→translate + full article over bundling the landing page / landing-page-first; the `red_flag`-beside-verbatim honesty model + the build-boundary full-article inline signed off). Decisions in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

## Phase 24: Cross-corpus synthesis — a typology lens over the multi-source corpus — M7

Turn the 4-source / 2-jurisdiction corpus (42 derived across 46 publications) from 46 isolated documents into an analytical tool. Synthesis groups documents by TYPOLOGY and shows the COMBINED coverage across the cluster — the genuinely new insight being **no single advisory covers a typology; the combined corpus does** (uniquely enabled now that the corpus spans FinCEN + OFAC + FINTRAC, US + Canada). The payoff reuses the existing per-indicator coverage math as honest UNION arithmetic (already disclosed-illustrative under the always-on badge). THE LOAD-BEARING HONESTY GATE (ties to the Phase-18 precision-lift rejection): NO similarity %, NO overlap score, NO correlation/lift number. Show only (a) combined coverage as set arithmetic, (b) honest per-jurisdiction contribution counts ("FINTRAC enumerates N indicators FinCEN doesn't"), (c) every indicator traceable to its source doc + jurisdiction. THE SUBTRACTION CHOICE: the typology label is ONE new field kept as a SEPARATE committed `data/typology-map.json` (doc-id → typology, closed vocab) rather than editing 42 derived records — so all 4 source dirs + 42 derived records + the grounding core `derive_signals.py` stay BYTE-FROZEN. `build.py` merges the map + validates it at the build boundary (closed vocab + referential integrity + full live-doc coverage, fail-loud). Agent proposes the map; the deterministic build-boundary gate disposes; the user reviews. INTEGRATION SHAPE (approved): a group-by-typology view reachable from Select → pick a typology → its cross-jurisdiction cluster + combined coverage → drill into any doc's existing per-doc arc (the per-doc arc stays the spine). THE ONE REAL RISK — whether genuine cross-jurisdiction clusters exist: terrorist financing almost certainly spans both (FINTRAC TF OA ↔ FinCEN ISIS fin-2025-a001 + Iran fin-2024-a001) and ransomware spans doc-types within FinCEN, but breadth is unverified → T1 VERIFIES clusters before T2 builds any UI, with a degrade path. ## Phase 23: FINTRAC depth — grow source #4 with the remaining anchorable strategic-intel products (Operational Alerts + Operational Briefs) — M7

Grow the existing FINTRAC corpus source #4 (data/fintrac/, doc_type "FINTRAC") from 3 derived Operational Alerts to the full anchorable set of FINTRAC strategic-intelligence products carrying enumerated ML/TF indicators — Operational Alerts AND Operational Briefs. This is "Canadian depth" for the demo's audience (a Canadian bank). NO new source, NO non-negotiable change (FINTRAC's Crown-copyright non-commercial reproduction basis was established in Phase 22), NO architecture change, NO build.py/corpus.html STRUCTURAL edit — the SELECT menu, doc_type chips, and count line are already data-driven, so adding docs to the source flows through the merge automatically (only the harness's hardcoded counts + the docs' counts update). Reuse the Phase-20 CORPUS_SOURCES registry + the inverted-loop derivation + the quote-grounding gate; the grounding core (normalize/check_record) stays BYTE-UNTOUCHED — only the rf_region relevance anchors widen. THE ONE REAL RISK — heterogeneous heading forms: the 3 derived OAs head their lists "Money laundering indicators" (ML/TF-qualified → anchors under the existing narrow `_RF_HEADER_FINTRAC`), but the real-estate Operational BRIEF was confirmed this session to head its ~40-indicator list with bare "TABLE OF INDICATORS", which the deliberately-narrow ML/TF-qualified FINTRAC anchor will NOT match. So the phase carries a REGRESSION-GATED anchor widening for each new FINTRAC heading form discovered (e.g. "TABLE OF INDICATORS"; possibly an inverted "Indicators of money laundering/TF" form). Each new anchor must be narrow + specific + verified 0-collision across ALL 36 FinCEN+OFAC mds, every existing rf_region BYTE-UNCHANGED, all 35 existing records `--check-derived` clean. The user chose this narrow-global-anchor widening (the proven Phase-21/22 pattern) OVER a cleaner source-scoped rf_region refactor (deferred — a future option if heading forms proliferate). Candidate batch (final set finalized at acquisition by what anchors + grounds): human-trafficking / Project Protect (oai-hts-2021), online child sexual exploitation / Project Shadow (exploitation), romance fraud / Project Chameleon, illegal wildlife trade, casino / underground-banking / Project Athena, and the real-estate Operational Brief (real). Honest yield ~5-8 → FINTRAC source 3 → ~8-11, becoming the second-deepest source. EXCLUDE FINTRAC's "Money laundering and terrorist financing indicators—<sector>" GUIDANCE pages (compliance guidance, NOT strategic-intelligence products — they would blur the corpus identity). Direction approved by user 2026-06-06 (FINTRAC depth at the goal gate — audience = a Canadian bank; OAs + Briefs WITH the regression-gated narrow-anchor widening at the scope gate, over OAs-only-no-gate-touch / the source-scoped refactor). ## Phase 22: FINTRAC as corpus source #4 (first cross-jurisdiction source; gate widened for FINTRAC "indicators" vocab; Crown-copyright non-commercial reproduction) — M7

Add FINTRAC (Canada's FIU, Financial Transactions and Reports Analysis Centre of Canada) as the FOURTH corpus source in the multi-source explorer — the demo's FIRST move beyond US-federal (US Treasury FinCEN + OFAC → +Canada FINTRAC). Reuse the Phase-20 CORPUS_SOURCES registry, the inverted-loop derivation, and the quote-grounding gate; the grounding core (normalize/check_record) stays BYTE-UNTOUCHED. Six lite tasks, subtraction-first / riskiest-first (T1 the gate change; the abort rule keys off it), mirroring Phase 21 (OFAC). What is genuinely NEW vs OFAC: (1) FIRST cross-jurisdiction source — a DIFFERENT compliance basis (Crown-copyright NON-COMMERCIAL reproduction LICENCE, NOT public domain — distinct from US-federal 17 USC §105); (2) gate vocab widening for FINTRAC's "indicators" heading (vs FinCEN "red flags" / OFAC "risk indicators"), deliberately NARROW (ML/TF-specific) to avoid shifting fin-2020-a008's "Financial/Behavioral Indicators" region; (3) source-aware attribution rendering (FINTRAC must NEVER show the US-federal "public domain" string). Verified this session: FINTRAC Operational Alerts (OAs) carry ~30–35 enumerated bulleted indicators (a clean derivation surface); FINTRAC's Terms & Conditions explicitly permit verbatim NON-COMMERCIAL reproduction WITH attribution; the narrow FINTRAC "indicators" anchor has 0 collisions across all 36 existing mds. Frozen sources (data/fincen/, data/fincen-alerts/, data/ofac/) + the showcase prove source #4 via the MERGE, not a migration. Direction approved by user 2026-06-06 (FINTRAC over the 3 documented next-source directions — "this demo is for a Canadian bank after all"; VERBATIM-corpus over paraphrase-corpus / paraphrase-showcase; the Crown-copyright non-commercial reproduction extension + the approach signed off "proceed"). Decisions in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

## Phase 21: OFAC as corpus source #3 (gate widened for OFAC vocab; US-federal public-domain) — M7

Add OFAC (US Treasury, Office of Foreign Assets Control) as the THIRD corpus source in the multi-source explorer, WITH a regression-gated widening of the rf_region anchors so OFAC's heading vocabulary derives. The user chose "widen the gate" over a small-clean-source (red-flag-template OFAC only, no gate change) or holding OFAC. EVIDENCE (planning probe): OFAC's red-flag-TEMPLATE advisories already ground in the UNCHANGED gate (the Sham Transactions advisory: rf_region (82,236), triage clean·26, heading "Red Flags: Indicia of Sham Transactions"), but most OFAC docs use "Risk Indicators" (VC guidance — "examples of risk indicators may be … who:"), "Deceptive Practices" (maritime), or defer red flags to a co-issued FinCEN advisory (ransomware → no list). rf_region is hard-coupled to the literal "red flag(s)" phrase (4 regexes — _RF_INTRO/_RF_HEADER/_RF_HEADER_LOOSE/_RF_INTRO_WEAK) and check_record treats rf_region==None as a HARD violation. So Phase 21 widens the anchor set to also match "Risk Indicators"/"Deceptive Practices"/"Risk Factors" headings (+ the risk-indicator intro), GATED by ZERO FinCEN regression. Regression risk is low (verified: "risk indicators" 0 FinCEN mds, "deceptive practic" 0, "risk factors" 1; 0 derived records mention them) — the new anchors are essentially inert for FinCEN docs, so their rf_regions stay byte-identical. COMPLIANCE: extend the verbatim public-domain non-negotiable from FinCEN-only to US-federal (17 USC §105 — FinCEN + OFAC + US Treasury/federal agencies), STILL excluding FINTRAC (Canadian Crown copyright) and any non-US/non-federal-government source (still paraphrase); the user signed off. ACQUISITION: OFAC's site is a JS SPA with no static advisories listing to crawl, so data/ofac/index.json is HAND-CURATED from OFAC's public-domain /media/<id>/download PDFs (no OFAC crawler — crawl_fincen.py stays FinCEN-only); acquire_fincen.py/pdf_to_md.py are reused via --source data/ofac + a small _to_pdf_url tweak to treat an absolute /media/.../download (and /system/files/) URL as a direct download. The hardcoded "FinCEN {advisory_no}" issuer in derive_signals.py corpus_status_records is parameterized (per-source issuer: FinCEN / OFAC). OFAC content is sanctions/vessel/compliance-oriented → records will be honestly enrichment/source-data-heavy with possibly 0 BUILD_NOW where the content is non-FI-observable (never fabricate a BUILD_NOW). Register OFAC as CORPUS_SOURCES source #3 (doc_type "OFAC"); corpus.html's menu chip + count line become 3-type-aware (the doc_type chip is already data-driven; only the non-Alert→"advisory" count bucket at corpus.html:276-277 needs fixing). The multi-source registry (Phase 20) was purpose-built for this — source #3 is a registry entry + a new data dir. Existing FinCEN sources (data/fincen/, data/fincen-alerts/) + the showcase stay byte-frozen; prove the 3rd source via the MERGE. Direction approved by user 2026-06-06 ("widen the gate for OFAC"; US-federal public-domain extension signed off; hand-curated OFAC acquisition; honest 0-BUILD_NOW allowed). Decisions in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

## Phase 20: Multi-source spine, proven with FinCEN Alerts — M7

Scale the corpus beyond FinCEN *advisories* to other FinCEN *publication types* (Alerts first), via a THIN multi-source pipeline registry — staying entirely inside the verbatim public-domain regime so NO non-negotiable changes (the "FinCEN-only verbatim" rail is untouched; still FinCEN, still 17 USC §105, still verbatim). The quote-grounding gate (derive_signals.py check_record / rf_region / normalize) is reused UNCHANGED — it is already source-agnostic. Architecture: introduce a thin SOURCES registry decoupling source-id from storage dir; build.py's render_corpus iterates sources, reads each source's corpus-status.json + derived/*.json, and merges by id into one __CORPUS__; derive_signals.py --corpus-status takes an optional source path to regenerate per-source. ZERO churn on what works: data/fincen/ stays the `fincen-advisories` source BYTE-UNCHANGED (no renaming the 14 mds + 12 derived + corpus-status); alerts land in a NEW data/fincen-alerts/ source. Proof: retarget acquisition to the FinCEN alerts hub (https://www.fincen.gov/resources/advisoriesbulletinsfact-sheets — alert PDFs are DIRECT zero-hop /system/files/...FinCEN Alert*.pdf links, simpler than advisories' detail-page resolution), acquire + markitdown-convert a batch → data/fincen-alerts/<id>.md, derive ~5 alerts that ground cleanly via the inverted loop (LLM extracts red flags + per-indicator judgment; --check-derived disposes), skip any alert with no red-flag region (rf_region None → non-derivable, honestly labeled like the 2 FATF advisories). corpus.html shows a UNIFIED menu across advisories + alerts with honest type chips (Advisory / Alert); the 5-screen arc is unchanged per record. The long tail of remaining alerts is cheap follow-on, not this phase. Network egress + markitdown both confirmed working in-session. Direction approved by user 2026-06-06 (scale via OTHER FinCEN publication types — Alerts first — over other agencies / a new showcase typology; thin SOURCES registry with data/fincen/ byte-frozen; proof batch ~5 alerts with a convert-one-first checkpoint; OFAC documented as the next-source candidate). Decisions in _CURRENT_STATE (lite).

## Phase 19: Durability closeout — commit corpus-explorer test harness + pin _rf_triage — M7

A DURABILITY CLOSEOUT at the end of the completed M0–M7 roadmap (the demo is at Definition of Done). User chose "durability closeout" over call-it-done / showcase-debt-true-up / new-stakeholder-ask: roadmap complete + at DoD, no net-new feature value remains without fabrication risk (combination-lift needs a precision source the records don't carry — rejected Ph18) and no scale frontier inside FinCEN (12/14 derived; the 2 FATF advisories have no red-flag list). The ONE remaining piece with real, non-fabrication value is DURABILITY — a committed test harness protecting the corpus.html/build.py investment, flagged as a SOFT OBSERVATION three phases running (Ph17/Ph18 journals). Two carried Phase-19 candidates resolved during planning: the `anthropic` pin is DEAD (`requirements-authoring.txt` does not exist — Phase 17's deletion already took it), and the `tests/` gap is REAL (`tests/` has only `fixtures/` + a manual `smoke-checklist.md`; the headless DOM-shim that verified the corpus explorer across Ph17/Ph18 is ad-hoc/uncommitted). The corpus explorer's JS is a single inline `<script>` (corpus.html ~189–537, ~350 lines) with a tiny DOM surface (getElementById, querySelectorAll, requestAnimationFrame, matchMedia, setTimeout, innerHTML-driven; no layout reads, no real events) → a hand-rolled ZERO-DEP Node DOM-shim is feasible, matching the project's dep-free `--selftest`/`--check` idiom; NEVER add a runtime DOM lib (jsdom) — the ship artifact is file:// offline. The `_rf_triage` footgun (Ph17 reviewer MEDIUM) is reframed: a REAL fix (accurate glued-no-separator counting) would REINTRODUCE exactly the deterministic parser Phase 17 deleted (anti-subtraction — glued advisories can't be reliably counted deterministically, which is WHY the inverted loop has the LLM read them), and it is harmless today (all 12 live advisories render from their records; build.py ignores flag_count for live ones; the 2 FATF non-derivable show count 0). So the durability-honest move is PIN + DISCLOSE, NOT rewrite. The UNFREEZE (only edits allowed): `tests/**`, `scripts/derive_signals.py` (comment + selftest fixture ONLY — no output change), `README.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `tests/smoke-checklist.md`. FROZEN byte-untouched: `index.html`, `corpus.html`, `scripts/build.py`, `config/**`, `data/fincen/**` (incl. corpus-status.json + derived/*.json), ALL of `dist/**` (the harness only READS dist/corpus/index.html). NO behavioral change to any shipped artifact — corpus-status.json and dist/** stay byte-identical; `--check all` must still show zero drift + the frozen set git-diff-empty. Direction approved by user 2026-06-06 (durability closeout over call-it-done / showcase-debt-true-up / new-stakeholder-ask; zero-dep Node DOM-shim; _rf_triage pin-not-rewrite).

## Phase 18: Corpus explorer arc — human gate + close-the-loop coverage payoff — M7

Give the corpus explorer (`dist/corpus/`, built from `corpus.html`) the dramatic arc the six-act showcase already has — grounded ENTIRELY in existing data, with NO fabricated numbers. The explorer today is a flat 4-screen analytical flow (Select → Coverage → Build recs → Signal) that ends on a spec card: no human-decision beat, no payoff. Add the two beats it lacks, mirroring the showcase's Act 3 (human gate) + Act 6 (loop closes). NEW ARC = 5 screens: (1) Select [unchanged] · (2) Coverage [unchanged] · (3) Build recs → THE GATE [BUILD_NOW recommendation rows become SELECTABLE — the human-in-the-loop "agent proposes, human disposes" thesis; folded INTO the existing Build-recs screen, not a new screen (subtraction test); default all-BUILD_NOW-selected, non-BUILD_NOW rows read-only; div-toggles NOT `<input>` so they can't collide with Space/arrow keyboard nav (the showcase `.selrow` pattern), preserving determinism/stage-reliability] · (4) Signal [spec cards for selected ∩ BUILD_NOW with build_logic; honest labeled empty state if 0 picked / 0 buildable] · (5) Close the loop [NEW — the coverage index animates BEFORE → AFTER, where "after" flips the picked gap indicators to covered via the existing `coverageIndex()` recompute (identical model to showcase Act 6); REDUCED-motion jumps to the after value; 0 BUILD_NOW / 0 picked → honest flat-hold + note, NEVER a fake rise]. CONTAINED SCOPE: the arc consumes only existing data fields (indicator status → coverage; build_rec → which are BUILD_NOW; build_logic → the spec card) → NO schema change, NO derived-record change, NO build.py change. The unfreeze is `corpus.html` ONLY (+ rebuild `dist/corpus` + docs). FROZEN/byte-untouched: `index.html`, `scripts/build.py`, `config/**`, `data/fincen/**` (corpus-status.json + derived/*.json), the 3 typology dists (`dist/{fentanyl,trade-based,elder-financial-exploitation}/`). The COVERAGE close-the-loop is the payoff, NOT precision combination-lift — precision-lift was EXPLICITLY REJECTED (the corpus records carry no precision/lift numbers; porting it would FABRICATE ~12 per-advisory illustrative stats, violating the "never present synthetic numbers as real" non-negotiable). Net-new buy-in value at the end of the completed M0–M7 roadmap, after a UX-research review confirmed the agentic spine was already sound. Direction approved by user 2026-06-06 (corpus-explorer arc over call-it-done / engineering-hygiene / showcase-true-up / deepening-the-showcase-gate; coverage payoff over precision-lift; corpus.html-only scope; div-toggle gate; gate folds into the Build-recs screen).

## Phase 17: Complete corpus derivation + delete extract_red_flags (the real subtraction) — M7

Finish the Phase-16 inversion thesis with the REAL line-count subtraction, plus scale the corpus explorer toward completion. Two halves, sequenced subtraction-first. (A) THE SUBTRACTION (do first — riskiest, highest-value, abort-rule keys off it): the debrief named "delete extract_red_flags" as the top candidate but had the dependency backwards — extract_red_flags is off the correctness path (the LLM extracts, the gate disposes) yet it is the SOLE producer of the extraction/flag_count triage the byte-frozen corpus.html renders for every "derivable-but-not-yet-derived" advisory (the "clean · N flags · not yet derived" chip; corpus.html:281-284, build.py:373). So a naive delete would force reimplementing a flag-counter — complexity MOVES not shrinks (the Phase-16 abort-rule failure mode). The clean fix, also REQUIRED by the user's "corpus may grow later" answer: add a ~12-line rf_region-bounded triage counter that reuses the already-existing rf_region(md) span and counts candidate lines inside it. rf_region already solved "where is the red-flag region"; counting inside it is trivial vs the 130-line section-finder. The counter (a) replaces extract_red_flags' triage role so the deletion is clean, (b) keeps --corpus-status fully REGENERABLE for a future new advisory (honors "may grow later"), (c) is genuinely smaller. With the counter as the bridge, the subtraction goes WIDE: delete extract_red_flags AND the now-dead deterministic-scaffold + neural-draft authoring stack it feeds (--scaffold/--draft/--scaffold-derived: scaffold_config, write_scaffold, draft_judgment, write_draft, _few_shot, _apply_judgment, _derived_skeleton) — the inverted loop (LLM extracts → --check-derived gate disposes) already replaced all of it. derive_signals.py drops ~1202 → ~700, leaving exactly normalize + rf_region + check_record + the counter + --corpus-status + --selftest (gate-only). The spine becomes purely "LLM extracts, deterministic gate disposes." (B) SCALE TO COMPLETION (on the stripped spine): derive the 5 remaining derivable advisories via the inverted loop → 7/14 → 12/14 live (only the 2 FATF advisories fin-2020-a009 + fin-2021-a003 stay non-derivable). FROZEN (byte-untouched): index.html, corpus.html, config/**, scripts/build.py, the 3 typology dists — the counter preserves corpus-status.json's SHAPE so NO front-end/build edit. Direction approved by user 2026-06-06 (scale-to-complete + delete over scale-only / lean-freeze-and-delete; counter bridge; may-grow-later → no lean freeze).

## Phase 16: Invert extraction (LLM extracts, deterministic gate disposes) + scale as proof — M7

Apply the SUBTRACTION TEST to the extraction spine. The deterministic `extract_red_flags` has accreted format special-casing every phase (two-tier anchors → footnote-resume → citation signatures → glued-handling, ~130 lines of `_SECTION_STOP`/`_FOOTNOTE_STOP`/`_CITATION` machinery) and still only cleanly parses 7/14 advisories; we already concede "the spine ASSISTS, it does not AUTOMATE — complete records need LLM authoring." Evidence the user surfaced: the extractor's raw output is noise the LLM already cleans (fin-2025-a003 raw L499 = `"FINCEN ADVISORY A customer that is a U.S.-based escrow company receives funds from an unaffiliated, foreign-"` — running-header glued on front, hyphen-truncated mid-sentence; the clean `flag` text in the record was LLM-authored by stitching across the page break). So INVERT: the **LLM (this model session as backend) EXTRACTS candidate red flags; the deterministic layer becomes a GROUNDEDNESS GATE that DISPOSES**. This is "deterministic validators at boundaries over neural judges at the end" — LLM at the start (extraction, a generative task it is good at), deterministic validator at the boundary (the gate). Complexity relocates from brittle section-PARSING (an open problem — every advisory's structure differs) to md NORMALIZATION (a closed problem — a finite known artifact set: running headers, form-feeds, hyphen-line-breaks, smart quotes, whitespace). Traceability moves from src_line (∈ extractor output) to VERBATIM QUOTE (normalized `flag` ∈ normalized md) — simpler AND stronger (checks actual text, format-agnostic); each derived-record indicator ALREADY carries the verbatim span in its `flag`, so migrating the 5 committed records is nearly free. matrix-consistency (`build_rec_category` cover×data), BUILD_NOW⇒full-build_logic, and shape checks are UNCHANGED — they stay deterministic dispose-logic. The named TRADE: groundedness proves a quote is REAL TEXT, not that it is a GENUINE red flag — decision A's cheap section-cite check buys back section-level relevance, the two human gates dispose on residual relevance. Standing non-negotiables PRESERVED: "LLM proposes, deterministic checks dispose" (strengthened — LLM now proposes extraction too); no authoring tool imported by index.html/build.py; ship artifact never calls an LLM (extraction is build-time, committed); derived records store RAW text, corpus.html `esc()` sole escaper; byte-frozen showcase (index.html, config/**, 3 typology dists — AND corpus.html, since corpus-status.json shape is preserved); deterministic layer stdlib-only, `import anthropic` lazy. Direction approved by user 2026-06-06 (invert the architecture + scale as proof; glued-converter option DISSOLVED).

## Phase 15: Harden extraction faithfulness + fix shipped defects — M7

Spine robustness, scoped by MEASUREMENT (not assumption). The LOW/NEEDS advisories fail for 3 distinct reasons: (a) footnote-interruption — a real, well-formed list hard-stopped mid-list by a page-boundary footnote run, orphaning everything after (fin-2025-a003 L499 escrow flag; fin-2025-a001 ISIS); (b) glued-no-separator — markitdown dropped both bullets AND blank lines so flags+intro-caveat fuse into one block (fin-2021-a004 ransomware, fin-2026-a001 health-care) — NO safe deterministic split signal; (c) partial/noise (fin-2021-a001, fin-2024-a001). The extractor's contract is extract-or-honestly-flag and it already honors (b)/(c); the only TRUE defect is the SILENT MISS in a CLEAN advisory (fin-2025-a003 reports CLEAN but drops L499 — a faithfulness lie) + the shipped fin-2022-a001 esc() double-escape render bug. Fix the true defects; do NOT build a brittle splitter. Glued-no-separator splitting DEFERRED (investigated, no safe signal → stays FLAGGED, re-confirming the Phase-12 flag-don't-force decision). Direction approved by user 2026-06-05 (harden spine + fix defects; defer glued-splitting).

## Phase 14: Scale corpus derivation (3 more CLEAN advisories → 5/14 live) — M7

Pure AUTHORING, no engineering: the spine tools (`--scaffold-derived`, `--check-derived`, `build.py corpus`) and the front-end (`corpus.html`) already exist from Phases 12–13. build.py makes an advisory "live" in the corpus explorer simply by the presence of `data/fincen/derived/<id>.json` — so deriving 3 more CLEAN advisories + rebuilding raises the explorer's live menu from 2/14 to 5/14, with ZERO changes to index.html, corpus.html, config/**, scripts/**, or the 3 typology dists. Per advisory, the proven loop: `--scaffold-derived <id> <md>` → author per-indicator judgment (status/data → build_rec via the cover×data matrix → rationale; build_logic for each BUILD_NOW gap) → PRUNE residual extraction noise → `--check-derived` passes (matrix-consistent + every indicator traces to a red-flag md line + BUILD_NOW⇒full definition). EFE (fin-2022-a002, 24) excluded — already the full showcase elder typology; COVID EIP (fin-2021-a002, 7) deferred — dated. Direction approved by user 2026-06-05 (scale derivation over spine-robustness / corpus-wow-beat / showcase-debt; 3 advisories, EFE/COVID out).

The six-act engine `index.html`, the 3 typology configs, and their dists stay BYTE-UNTOUCHED — `corpus.html` owns its own copy of the dossier theme CSS (no shared include). build.py stays DECOUPLED from the authoring layer: it reads committed data artifacts (corpus-status.json + derived/*.json) and never imports derive_signals.py. ## Phase 12: FinCEN corpus derivation foundation (deterministic spine all-14 + LLM proof slice) — COMPLETED + accepted (impl commit 90939b4)

Build the backend for an EXPANDED, singular FinCEN demo (eventual: user picks one of 14 advisories, watches the loop derive its coverage → build recommendations → signal). This phase = the **deterministic spine validated across all 14** + the **LLM-backend (this session, NO API key) derivation proven on a 2–3 advisory slice**. Boundary preserved (Phase-11 principle, extended): the LLM proposes the build recommendation + build logic; the deterministic spine disposes (schema/shape · build-rec consistency vs cover×data · traceability to a red-flag md line). The 14 corpus md's are ALREADY on disk (gitignored) + network is blocked in-session — so no acquisition; T1 commits the existing corpus. Demo expansion (advisory-selection UI + build-rec render) deferred to Phase 13. Engine `index.html` + `build.py` UNTOUCHED (backend-only). Direction approved by user 2026-06-05 (backend-only over a minimal selectable view).

## Phase 11: Automated derivation (LLM-drafted signal config) — AUTOMATE — COMPLETED + accepted (impl commit c37dc39)

Automate the **article→signal derivation** step proven MANUALLY in Phase 7 (EFE md → hand-derived elder config). One new authoring-only tool `scripts/derive_signals.py`, two layers: a **deterministic layer** (stdlib-only, offline, `--selftest`/`--scaffold`) that extracts the FinCEN red flags and scaffolds a schema-shaped config SKELETON, + a **neural layer** (`--draft`, env-keyed) that proposes the judgment fields (status per indicator, exactly one `target:true` indicator + candidate, the signal `definition`) by calling the Anthropic API. **Boundary preserved:** the LLM PROPOSES a `config/typologies/<id>.draft.json`; the deterministic validator (`build.py` + schema) + the two human gates DISPOSE — no neural judge at the build boundary, committed configs stay deterministic + human-reviewed. `derive_signals.py` is NEVER imported by `index.html`/`build.py`; the ship artifact never calls an LLM (HANDOFF §4/§4.5 hold). The `anthropic` SDK lives in the gitignored authoring venv (markitdown precedent), LAZY-imported only inside `--draft`. Direction approved by user 2026-06-05 (AUTOMATE → variant B over the planner's finish-first + deterministic-only recommendations).

## Phase 10: FinCEN corpus crawler (SCALE) — COMPLETED + accepted (commit 0c87c47)

Widen the authoring scraper from one hand-registered advisory to the **discovered FinCEN advisory corpus**. The static 1-entry `REGISTRY` in `acquire_fincen.py` becomes a **generated manifest** (`data/fincen/index.json`: `{id, title, date, type, url}`). Core value is **discovery**, not mass-download: a new authoring-only `crawl_fincen.py` with a **pure `parse_index(html)→entries`** (deterministic, `--selftest` against a saved fixture — same ethos as Phase-9 `build.py --check`) + a **thin live `fetch_index()` shell** (manual authoring run, never in smoke/CI). The committed manifest is produced by `parse_index(saved_fixture)` so it's reproducible **offline**. The widened pipe is proven on a **bounded batch (~2–3)**, not the whole corpus. Engine/ship artifact untouched; `raw/` stays gitignored; commit the manifest + only the md we derive from — no bulk-md commit. Direction approved by user 2026-06-05.

- [x] T1 · Discovery probe + saved fixture | scope: tests/fixtures/** | success: the real FinCEN advisories index page is saved to `tests/fixtures/fincen-index.html` (manual authoring fetch) and its source URL recorded (comment/README line); the fixture is non-empty and lists multiple advisories with links + dates. VERIFY: `test -s tests/fixtures/fincen-index.html && [ "$(grep -ciE '/system/files|advisor' tests/fixtures/fincen-index.html)" -ge 5 ]`. ABORT: if the index is JS-rendered / requires auth / blocks scraping so no deterministic parser can be built from a saved page — PAUSE and pivot to a hand-curated manifest (still an upgrade over the 1-entry stub) | size: S ✓ PASS (fixture 49KB, server-rendered HTML table; 14 advisories `fin-2020-a008`…`fin-2026-a001` incl. EFE `fin-2022-a002`; provenance-stamped; source `…/advisoriesbulletinsfact-sheets/advisories`; VERIFY 60≥5. ABORT not triggered. DISCOVERY below)

> DISCOVERY (T1, escape-hatch — refines T2/T3): the listing yields **detail-page URLs** (`/resources/advisories/fincen-advisory-fin-YYYY-aXXX`, id in the slug) + title (`<a>` text) + a `MM/DD/YYYY` release-date column — NOT direct PDF URLs (FinCEN PDF filenames are unpredictable, e.g. EFE `…FINAL%20508.pdf`). So manifest `url` = the **detail page** (deterministic from the listing → offline-reproducible); resolving the actual PDF is an **acquire-time hop** (fetch detail page → regex the `/system/files/…pdf` link). The static EFE entry (direct PDF) stays merged as a fallback/override so `acquire fin-2022-a002` is zero-hop + backward-compatible.

## Phase 9: Build-drift guard (zero-drift invariant) — COMPLETED + accepted

Turn the M5 zero-drift invariant (committed `dist/<id>/index.html` == fresh build) — which silently broke in Phase 7, caught only by accident in Phase 8 — into a runnable, non-mutating guard wired into the smoke-checklist. Doc + build-script-glue only; engine (`index.html`) untouched, no config changes → all 3 `dist/` stay byte-identical. Mechanism = in-process `build.py --check` (render + byte-compare vs committed dist), pure-stdlib + git-agnostic. Direction approved by user 2026-06-05.

- [x] T1 · Add `--check` drift guard to build.py | scope: scripts/build.py | success: refactor extracts `render_one(typ, template) -> str` (validate + inline + self-contained guard, returns the output string) with a thin writer calling it; `main` accepts `--check` with an optional `all`/`<id>` arg; the check path byte-compares each render against the committed `dist/<id>/index.html`, prints a per-typology drift report, exits 0 when all match and non-zero on any mismatch or missing built file. VERIFY: `python3 scripts/build.py --check all` exits 0 on clean HEAD; a trivial un-rebuilt edit to one config makes it exit non-zero NAMING that typology (then revert); `python3 scripts/build.py all && git diff --exit-code dist/` clean (refactor output-neutral); `node --check` PASS on a freshly built dist | size: S
- [x] T2 · Wire guard into smoke-checklist + fix stale prose | scope: tests/smoke-checklist.md | success: the manual zero-drift prose item (≈L72, currently "build.py all reproduces both dist byte-identical") is replaced with the runnable `python3 scripts/build.py --check all` command; the stale "both dist"/2-typology count is corrected to all 3 typologies (fentanyl, trade-based, elder-financial-exploitation); the `git status --porcelain dist/` complement is noted. VERIFY: `grep -n "build.py --check all" tests/smoke-checklist.md` hits; `grep -niE "both dist|both typolog" tests/smoke-checklist.md` returns nothing | size: S
- [x] T3 · Document `--check` usage | scope: scripts/build.py, README.md | success: build.py module docstring Usage block lists the `--check [all|<id>]` mode with a one-line description; README "How to run" carries a one-line drift-guard command. VERIFY: `grep -n "\-\-check" scripts/build.py README.md` hits both files | size: S

> Exit (phase-09): `build.py --check all` exits 0 on clean HEAD, non-zero + names the typology on un-rebuilt drift · `build.py all && git diff --exit-code dist/` clean (output-neutral) · smoke-checklist carries the runnable `--check all` guard, no stale "both dist"/2-typology count, 3 typologies + `git status --porcelain dist/` complement · `--check` in build.py docstring + README · `git diff index.html` empty.
